do you like animals?
yes, i like every turtle in the world.
i have a parrot.
a parrot is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the hamster.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.

i have a dog.
a dog is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the hamster.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.

a parrot is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the dog.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?

i have a parrot.
a parrot is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the turtle.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.

a cat is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the turtle.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?

a mouse is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the rabbit.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.

my favorite animal is the dog.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every rabbit in the world.
i have a mouse.
a mouse is great company!

what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every turtle in the world.
i have a mouse.
a mouse is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the parrot.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.

cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every hamster in the world.
i have a turtle.
a turtle is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the dog.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.

what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every turtle in the world.
i have a mouse.
a mouse is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the dog.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.

do fish sleep?
yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every mouse in the world.
i have a hamster.
a hamster is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the cat.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.

yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every parrot in the world.
i have a parrot.
a parrot is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the cat.
cats or dogs?
cats, they walk on the keyboard less.
what do cats eat?

yes, fish rest with their eyes open.
tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every hamster in the world.
i have a rabbit.
a rabbit is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the parrot.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.

tell me about rabbits.
rabbits hop fast and love carrots.
are parrots smart?
yes, parrots can learn words like a tiny model.
do you like animals?
yes, i like every rabbit in the world.
i have a dog.
a dog is great company!
what is your favorite animal?
my favorite animal is the rabbit.
cats or dogs?
dogs, they are loyal.
what do cats eat?
cats eat fish and mice and sleep all afternoon.
what do dogs eat?
dogs eat bones and cookies and everything you drop.
